# Chapter 6: Dwellers of Terra

AK5e is quite a different game to typical Fifth edition D&D as a result of the mechanical changes made, and the source material on which it is based. 
:
In Arknights, you fight hordes of enemies with a comparative handful; in Dungeons and Dragons, it is rare that you fight more than a dozen or so enemies simultaneously, and usually specific enemies, such as zombies or goblins. Even putting aside the narrative aspect for a moment, the reason why it is these sorts of Creatures that tend to be encountered in hordes is their simplicity. Goblins, in essence, have a single "game-plan", and do not require much cognitive load on the DM's behalf to run, whether there is 5 or 50. 
:
It is with this in mind that Creatures in AK5e are designed, using the same categories as Arknights itself. Creatures in AK5e are sorted into one of three categories:
:
##### Normal
Normal Creatures are very simple. They are designed to be run in large numbers; typically, they do a single thing mechanically, such as move and attack. They also have fewer Hit Points than would be expected at their CR, though their Damage output remains the same. Because they are expected to be run in groups, to tone down the disproportionate impact of numbers on the amount of critical hits, <u>Normal Creatures cannot score Critical Hits</u>. Additionally, to make them easier to run in those large numbers, they <u>automatically fail all Saving Throws.</u>

##### Elite
Elite Creatures have a higher degree of complexity, and may have a "game-plan" with steps, or have options and choices of what to do. Elite Creatures also typically have much more Hit Points than Normal Creatures. They are designed to be encountered in much smaller numbers than Normal Creatures, perhaps accompanying groups of Normal Creatures as a sort of lieutenant or champion. 

##### Leader
Leader Creatures have a high degree of complexity. With a few notable exceptions, Leader Creatures are only ever meant to be encountered one at a time, and often alone. Many Leader Creatures will also have Legendary Actions or Lair Actions.

\column
:
{{note
##### Swarms of Normal Creatures
Running large groups of even simple Creatures can be very difficult. Dungeons and Dragons is a game with many moving parts, and increasing the number of those parts from 10 to 50, even if they are very simple parts, can have a big impact on the GM's cognitive load. These are the suggestions for converting a Normal Creature into a Swarm of that Creature; a single Creature to represent many. 
:
A Creature converted to a Swarm gains the following Trait:
:
**Swarm.** This is a Swarm, which has the following effects:
- This Swarm can occupy another Creature’s space and vice versa, and the Swarm can move through any opening large enough for the base Creature's size. 
- When any effect that targets this Swarm would be required to target a different Creature, or another Creature, the Swarm can be chosen as that additional target. 
- If this Swarm has less than half of its maximum Hit Points remaining, the Damage it deals is halved. 
- The Swarm has Advantage on Saving Throws against effects that only target a single Creature.
- When an effect or Art that affects an area such as a cone, cylinder, sphere, or cube would deal Damage to a Swarm, that Swarm takes the maximum possible Damage that could have been rolled; roll for Damage against all other Creatures normally.

{{align-text:left
A Swarm also gains additional benefits based on the size of the Swarm, which is different to the size of the Creatures that comprise it. A Swarm's Hit Points are calculated by multiplying the Hit Points of the Creature it is based on; when a Swarm deals Damage, it deals additional Damage based on its size; when a Swarm takes the Attack Action, it can make a number of additional Attacks based on its size.
}}

##### 
| Swarm Size | Hit Point Multiplier | Additional Damage Dice | Additional # of Attacks |
|:-----------|:--------------------:|:----------------------:|:-----------------------:|
| Tiny       | x3                   | 0                      | 1
| Small      | x4                   | 1                      | 1
| Medium     | x5                   | 1                      | 2
| Large      | x6                   | 2                      | 2
| Huge       | x7                   | 2                      | 3
| Gargantuan | x8                   | 3                      | 3
:
Consider that the space a Swarm occupies will be based not just on the size of the Swarm, but also the size of the Creature; a small Swarm of Medium Creatures will occupy more space than a small Swarm of Tiny Creatures!

}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

## DM Advice: Designing Encounters for AK5e
:
#### Normal Creatures
As you read through the following section, you will note that Normal Creatures have very low CR; the highest CR possessed by a normal Creature in this book is 2, with most being somewhere between 1/4 and 1. This is intentional; just as Arknights is a game designed around fighting lots of weak enemies backed up by stronger enemies, so is AK5e. 
:
While this will change drastically based on the tactical situation, the following table describes what Level is expected for a balanced party of four to have a hard difficulty encounter against ten Normal Creatures of that CR.

| Character Level   | Enemy CR | 
|:-----------------:|:--------:|
| 1  | 0 
| 2  | 1/8
| 3  | 1/4
| 5  | 1/2
| 7  | 1
| 11 | 2

{{align-text:left
From this, you can adjust expectations accordingly as to how many enemies to add. Remember that as the DM you have executive power over the strongest tool in your arsenal; adjusting things as you go along. If you feel an encounter is too easy, you can increase the number of enemies by having new ones enter the battle. If you feel you have overestimated the players, have the morale of the enemies break after they have beaten a certain amount and the rest flee, saving them from having to fight the rest. 
}}

#### Elite Creatures
Elite Creatures are appropriately scaled to battle characters of a Level approximate to their CR, and should be used appropriately. Generally speaking, Elite Creatures should appear alongside Normal Creatures, though they can of course appear alongside other Elites for more personal battles. When using Elite Creatures alongside Normal Creatures, consider using an Elite of a slightly lower CR than the level of the Characters to balance out the additional Actions their "team" has each Round. If the Elite Creature is facing Characters alone, try to use an Elite Creature of a CR the same or slightly higher than the level of the Characters. 

\column

#### Leader Creatures
Leader Creatures are mostly designed to be encountered alone without support, and are strong enough to compete with a party of four characters of roughly the same level on an even playing field. There are some exceptions; Creatures like the Royal Court Captain or Elite Imperial Striker benefit allied Creatures around them, and so they are less powerful if they do not have supporting Creatures. Generally speaking, if a Leader Creature has Traits or Actions that benefit surrounding allies, but cannot call them or summon them itself, it should have some Creatures with it during the encounter. Many Leader Creatures will have the ability to call allies to themselves, and have large pools of Hit Points and sometimes multiple "phases". 
:
When using a Leader Creature, think carefully about the tactical situation; a Leader Creature of the same CR as the Character's Level may be a fair fight if they have resources to use, but may be a very unfair encounter against Characters that are drained of resources.

## Index of Creature Stat Blocks
:
Stat Blocks of Creatures in this section are organised by Faction alphabetically, then by Creature category, then finally by increasing Challenge Rating.

### Collapsals
Collapsal is the blanket term for the various different types of extra-dimensional invader that can be encountered at Terra's poles, as well as those overtaken by their corrosive influence. 

Collapsals have a deleterious effect on reality surrounding them, possibly due to their nature as half-real, half-unreal beings. In their presence, conventional physics and cognition begins to break down, making them incredibly dangerous. Collapsals are almost impossible to harm, but only seem to be able to interact with the physical world when directly observed. They are extremely hostile to all forms of life and even machinery, but are rarely capable of causing direct harm. Instead, they attempt to corrupt whatever they can as soon as they can, utilizing illusion and mental interference. 

In addition, Collapsal influence has a permanent effect on the world; in the areas where they have been permitted to corrupt, "contamination" can spread; appearing as a black fog, it is far more insidious, representing the overwriting of consensus reality by the Collapsals. Exposure to this corruption is just as harmful as exposure to the Collapsals themselves, causing corruption.

{{imageMaskEdge8,--offset:9%,--rotation:-12

##### Shattered Champion
The Treescar Tribe are known as one of the mightiest warrior tribes in Sami, and they have committed themselves to combatting the demons from the north. But no-one is invincible, and no-one does not have moments of weakness. Clad in Sami-made armour, and wielding the fearsome hammers of the Treescar, they are formidable opponents.

##### Warped Golem
Sami Snowpriests are sometimes known to use a combination of Arts and prayer to Samivillin to awaken the land to defend them in dire circumstances; the very trees and stones will march when several Snowpriests work in tandem. For a time, it was thought that these constructs were immune to Collapse; a falsehood soon laid bare.

#### Leader
:
##### "Eikthyrnir," the Scar of the Shattered
Once the chief of the Treescar Tribe, Sami's foremost defenders against the Collapsal threat. He never once felt fear, never once stepped back from a foe or his duty. His defence never wavered, even as his husk has become a threat to the very land he once guarded, waging guerilla warfare on the men he once led.

***Brainbreakers.*** Whenever you would take a Reaction against something this Creature has done, you must first succeed on a DC13 Intelligence Saving Throw. If the Saving Throw is failed, that Reaction is not performed, but your Reaction is not consumed.
:
***Legendary Resistance (3/Day).*** If Eikthyrnir fails a Saving Throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

### Actions
***Multiattack.*** Eikthyrnir makes two Melee Attacks. He can replace one of those Attacks with a use of Binding Ice.
:
***Binding Ice (Recharge 4+).*** Eikthyrnir casts Rime's Binding Ice at 5th Level, using Wisdom as the Spellcasting Ability (Spell Save DC 16)
:
***Treescar Greathammer.*** *Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack:* +8 to hit, reach 10ft. or range 30/50ft., one target, *Hit:* 15 (3d6+4) Bludgeoning Damage + 7 (2d6) Cold Damage
:
The Hammer immediately returns to Eikthyrnir's hand after making a Ranged Attack.

### Bonus Actions
***Hammer Throw.*** Eikthyrnir makes a Ranged Attack with the Treescar Hammer.
:
***Reality Bends (Recharge 4+).*** Eikthyrnir enters the Aetherside for a moment, becoming Intangible and Invisible for up to 1 minute. When he does so, his use of Binding Ice is immediately restored, and all other Conditions affecting him are ended. While Intangible, this Creature cannot deal or take Damage, and its Speed is doubled. Eikthyrnir may exit the Aetherside early, ending the Intangible and Invisible effects, by using Binding Ice.
:
This Action cannot Recharge while Eikthyrnir in in the Aetherside.

### Reactions
***Survive at all Costs (1/day).*** *Trigger:* This Creature would be reduced to 0 Hit Points. *Response:* This Creature instead survives with 1 Hit Point, immediately regains use of its Reality Bends Action, and can use that Action as part of this Reaction.

}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

##### Crazelyseon, the Eternal Echo
An unfathomable Collapsal that emerged from the northern aethergate, the apocalyptic threat posed by Crazelyseon is tempered only by its purely instinctual nature. Crazelyseon does not think, at least not in any way mortals could appreciate, and is more akin to a force of nature that a living being. Were it able to act with malice, Terra would surely be facing an unprecedented threat. Like most Collapsals, it exists in a state between the Aetherside and baseline reality, rendering it all but immune to conventional attacks. Thanks again to its thoughtless nature, it only ever dwells in the locations around the aethergate, where the technology of the First Civilisation still exists and can be used against it. 

}}

{{note,wide
##### Crazelyseon's Lair, the Aethergate
Crazelyseon MUST be encountered at its lair as it cannot leave the vicinity of the Aethergate. In the Lair there is a Dimensional Anchoring Pylon and three sub-pylons; each of these sub-pylons must be within 100 feet of the Anchoring Pylon, and must be accessible for the encounter to be fair. 
:
Each of the sub-pylons can be charged as an Action by a Humanoid Creature within 5 feet of it. Once all three Pylons are charged, the Dimensional Anchoring Pylon can be activated as a Bonus Action by a Humanoid Creature within 5 feet of it. When the Dimensional Anchoring Pylon is activated, it remains activated until the end of the next Turn of the Creature who activated it. 
:
While it is active, Crazelyseon loses its Damage Immunities. When it deactivates, all of the sub-pylons must be charged again before the Anchoring Pylon can be reactivated.

}}

{{monster,frame,wide
## Crazelyseon, the Eternal Echo
*Medium Aberration, Unaligned*
___
**Armor Class** :: 17 (Aetherflicker)
**Hit Points**  :: 229 (51d8+0)
**Speed**       :: Hover 0 ft.
___
|  STR  |  DEX  |  CON  |  INT  |  WIS  |  CHA  |
|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|
|10 (0) |10 (0) |10 (0) |1 (-5) |22 (+6)|20 (+5)|
___
**Saving Throws**        :: Wisdom +10, Charisma +9
**Damage Immunities**    :: All Damage
**Condition Immunities** :: All Conditions
**Senses**               :: passive Perception 16
**Languages**            :: -
**Challenge**            :: 11 (7200 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** +4}}
---

***Brainbreakers.*** Whenever you would take a Reaction against something this Creature has done, you must first succeed on a DC13 Intelligence Saving Throw. If the Saving Throw is failed, that Reaction is not performed, but your Reaction is not consumed.

### Actions
***Multiattack.*** Crazelyseon uses Spread Chaos and Corrupt.

\column

***Corrupt.*** All non-Aberration Creatures within Seedbed and within a 100-foot radius of Crazelyseon must make a DC 18 Charisma Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 22 (4d10) Psychic Damage; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage (11). 
:
A Creature that is reduced to 0 Hit Points by this Damage becomes a Collapsal; their Creature Type is changed to Aberration, they cannot speak or communicate, are immune to the Charmed and Frightened Conditions, and are allied to Crazelyseon. For all intents and purposes, they are dead, and cannot be revived.
:
***Spread Chaos.*** Crazelyseon spreads Seedbed in a 10-foot-radius sphere around itself. Seedbed is a form of Collapsal Corruption that appears to be the growing of intangible alien flora. Other Creatures can clear a 10-foot-radius of Seedbed around them by succeeding on a DC 18 Wisdom Check as an Action. If that Creature can cast Arts, they may use their Spellcasting Ability for the Check, and may also add their Proficiency Bonus to the roll. 

### Bonus Actions
***Hole in the World.*** Crazelyseon rolls 2d10 on the Hole in the World Table and summons the corresponding results to unoccupied spaces within 15 feet of Crazelyseon. The Creature Type of the summoned Creatures is changed to Aberration, they cannot speak or communicate, are immune to the Charmed and Frightened Conditions, and are allied to Crazelyseon.

### Legendary Actions
***Teleport.*** Crazelyseon teleports to a location within 100 feet; it cannot move further away from the Aethergate than 300 feet.
}}

### Creatures
Creatures are the beasts of Terra. While they are sometimes employed by opportunistic folk as battle animals, typically they are simply trying to survive; the act of which can lead to conflict with man. 

#### Normal
:
##### Beckbeast
Beckbeasts are a small canid creature found all across Terra, similar to a cross between a mole and a dog. They have an extremely good sense of smell, and can be trained, so are quite often employed in the military and law enforcement to sniff out contraband.

##### Originium Slug
Originium Slugs, despite what the name might imply, are not a transmission vector for Oripathy. They are large invertebrates that possess a symbiotic relationship with Originium ore, and express it in their outer shells, which can become quite tough as they grow. This Originium is not active, and is thus not infectious; in some places in the world, people hunt these slugs for their meat.

##### Needlefly
Needleflies are massive, territorial insects resembling dragonflies, with a massive horn-like protrusion on their head. This horn is a stinger, and is the way in which Needleflies inject their dangerous venom, stabbing deep with their horn during their rapid charges. Needlefly nests that are too close to civilisation are often the subject of extermination efforts considering the threat they pose to travellers especially.

{{wide

##### Rupturing Infected Originium Slug
While Originium Slugs do not typically contract Oripathy or become contaminated by active Originium, it can happen under extreme circumstances; a particularly contaminated Catastrophe, extreme pollution, or wilful tampering on someone's malicious behalf. Either way, these simple pests become extremely dangerous contamination vectors.

{{note
##### Note for DM; Oripathy Vector
Creatures like this are dangerous potential vectors of Oripathy. Depending on your stance, you may not wish to have Oripathy be something so mechanical; if you would rather not, ignore the Infection Vector Feature of this Creature. If you do decide to keep that feature, it is recommended to make your players aware of the danger.
}}

}}

##### Slumberfoot

\column

##### Metal Crab
Metal Crabs, as they are known, are a species of crustacean whose body is coated in the metal from the ore they regularly consume. This armour renders them all but immune to predation, and those that make it to adulthood can achieve prodigious size. They are herbivorous and dislike confrontation, but will swing their heavy bodies around if pressed. They are so heavy that falling over can harm them.

##### Gloompincer
Gloompincers are large freshwater crustaceans named for their powerful pincers. Their claws have a limited range of motion, only able to strike forward and down. Gloompincers can be territorially defensive, which becomes a problem when they have taken up residence in a sewer. In Kazimierz, captured Gloompincers are sometimes used as opponents for wannabe Knights in off-the-books competitions.

\column

##### Manglerbeast
Manglerbeasts are large, powerful furred canids that can be found across Terra. Similar in arrangement and stature to bears, they differ in two major ways; the prominent sabertooth-esque incisors that protrude from their upper jaw, and that they possess sequential hermaphrodism. All Manglers are female until approximately age ten, where some members of a pack will become male. This has an extremely detrimental effect on the now-male's lifespan, with many dying not long after breeding; a behaviour that has led to a common misconception that manglerbeasts can only be female.

##### Pompeii

***Slug Queen.***  :: Pompeii calls 11 (3d6) Originium Slugs or 4 (1d6) Acidic Originium Slugs to defend them; they appear in a location that makes sense, such as from deeper in the lair.
***Tectonic Tremble.***  :: A tremor shakes the ground in a 60-foot-radius around Pompeii. Each Creature other than Pompeii within the area must succeed on a DC 15 Strength Saving Throw or be knocked Prone.

\page

### Reunion
Reunion are a paramilitary organisation comprised of those with Oripathy. They were formed to oppose persecution of the Infected by nations, violently if required, and seek to end all such persecution and pursue their justice against its perpetrators. However, since the time of its foundation, it has undergone several internal ideological shifts regarding how radical members are willing to be, leading to a great many splinter groups, all of whom call themselves Reunion. 
:
Reunion's use of violence and guerilla warfare has earned them the label of "terrorists" from most Terran nations. Though, there are almost always some that are sympathetic to their goals, if not their means, and are willing to provide what aid they can. Despite splintering, despite schisms, despite purges; Reunion endures, and always will so long as the Infected continue to be oppressed.

#### Normal
:
##### Airborne Soldier
Airborne Soldiers are Reunion soldiers with perhaps more bravery than intelligence. Strapped to a hastily constructed backpack that allows them a few brief moments of unsafe flight, they strike into the backline of their oppressors as shock troops. 
:
{{monster,frame
## Airborne Soldier
*Medium Humanoid (any race), Chaotic Good*
___

{{note
##### Crossbowman Leaders
To create a Crossbowman Leader, use this stat block, but make the following changes: 

- Exchange their Salvaged Weapon for a Light Crossbow and a Knife.
- Swap their Strength and Dexterity Scores (this will also increase their AC to 15)
}}

:
***Raise the Herd (Art) (Recharge 5+).*** Mephisto chooses two Creatures they can see within 60 feet who fulfil the following conditions: not Undead, have died within the last hour, and had Oripathy. 

Those Creatures are returned to life with half of their maximum Hit Points. Their Creature Type becomes Undead, their Intelligence and Charisma Scores are reduced to 4, and they become Immune to the Frightened and Charmed Condition. Additionally, at the beginning of each of their Turns. they regain 1d8 Hit Points. However, these Creatures cannot cast Arts, use any Ability labelled as (Art), or make Ranged Attacks.
:
***Spellcasting.*** This Creature casts one of the following Arts, using Wisdom as its Spellcasting Ability (Spell Save DC 15):

*At Will:* Toll the Dead

*1/day each:* Aura of Vitality, Mass Healing Word

### Bonus Actions
***Command the Herd.*** Mephisto chooses a Creature he can see; until the start of his next Turn, allied Undead may move 10 additional feet if they are moving towards that Creature.


}}

##### Developer Notes: Mephisto and Faust
Mephisto and Faust are meant to be encountered together. Additionally, as Squad Leaders of Reunion, they are expected to be accompanied by other members of Reunion; Mephisto in particular may be underwhelming without targets for his Raise the Herd Action.

{{wide

##### Hateful Avenger

A mysterious warrior with a masked face, little is known about them. They serve as one of Reunion's squad leaders, but make no mistake; their burning blade is more of a threat than any nameless agitator. They seek ever greater battles and ever greater challenges, in service to one day achieve a vengeance they will not discuss. If you believe yourself a worthy challenger, then step forth.

{{note
##### Variant: Hateful Avenger, Invincible Blade
The Hateful Avenger gains the following trait:

***Vengeance, Flame and Steel (1/day).*** When this Creature would be reduced to 0 Hit Points but not killed outright, its current Hit Point total instead resets to 150. For the next hour, its Melee Attacks are made with Advantage, but it loses 11 (2d10) Hit Points at the end of each of its Turns; this Damage cannot reduce it below 1 Hit Point. Award an additional 4,100 XP (10,000 total) for defeating the Hateful Avenger after it has revived in this way.
}}

{{wide

##### FrostNova, Winter's Scar

Real name Yelena, the enigmatic leader of Reunion's Yeti Squadron has a host of nom de guerre fitting her terrifying prowess; Yeti Princess, Wintry Death, and of course, FrostNova. These names tell of the terrifying cold death that await those who oppose her, but fail to encompass who FrostNova actually is; a warm-hearted and caring leader of her men, who she sees as her brothers and sisters, and a true beacon of freedom for oppressed Infected worldwide. Unlike others in Reunion, FrostNova has never given into cruelty and tyranny, and never will.

}}

{{wide

##### Talulah Artorius, Tyrant of Reunion

The Talulah that got within a hairs-breadth of plunging the world into war, the Talulah who condemned the people of Chernobog city to death; this Talulah has not always existed. Once, Talulah was simply a strong-willed young woman, exiled from her home of Victoria by the coup against the Draco species she belongs to. She found a home for a time in Lungmen, before being kidnapped by Duke Kaschey of Ursus, who raised her as his heir. She rejected his teachings, even going so far as to murder the Duke and flee into the tundra. It is in this tundra that she formed Reunion after seeing the tragedy faced by Ursus Infected, and for a time, she was a symbol of hope. 
:
However, some teachings, some beliefs, stick deep; deeper than we could ever know. When tragedy struck, claiming the life of her best friend, just before she witnessed the ugly selfishness of the people she had fought to protect, Kaschey's callous teachings of dominance flooded to the fore. In those moments, her sword stained with the blood of those she saw as having betrayed her, did the light of the burning houses shine the last light on the face of Talulah, Freedom Fighter, and the first on the face of Talulah, Tyrant of Reunion. 

}}

{{wide

##### Patriot, The Last Wendigo

Buldrokkas'tee to few, Patriot to many, and the Aegis of the Infected to many more, Patriot is Reunion's mightiest warrior. Over two centuries of grinding conflict, first for Kazdel, then for Ursus, and finally for the Infected, have forged him into the ultimate spear and shield. He is a man for whom fatigue holds no sway, a man unmoved by his own terminal illness, a man who marches past the corpses of his loved ones even as his tears fall; he is Patriot, and he will always advance.
:
For Reunion, Patriot leads his Shieldguards, many of whom defected from Ursus alongside him, and have fought the Empire from the tundra for decades. When Patriot marches, so do they.

}}

### Sarkaz
Sarkaz is an umbrella term for a number of distinct species, separate from the Ancients; their history of war and strife is longer than the total history of some Terran nations. While many Sarkaz originate from the state of Kazdel, the events of the previous millennia have scattered the Sarkazian diaspora all over the world. Prejudice against the Sarkaz is not uncommon, and has become a self-fulfilling prophecy for some; discrimination against the Sarkaz due to them being perceived as violent thugs prevents them getting stable employment, leaving the only work available as mercenary work, which earns them as a reputation as a violent thug. 

Far from every Sarkaz is of martial bent, but there is no denying the talent of those that are. From the lowliest mercenary to the mightiest professional soldier of the Kazdel Royal Court, it is rare to find a battlefield on Terra without the Sarkaz being involved in it somehow.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/5/53/Sarkaz_Mercenary_C.png?2655ed&20240411044714' 
  style='position:absolute;top:420px;right:300px;width:600px' />

{{note
##### The Scar Market, Kazdel's Mercenary Hub
In Kazdel, the "home" of mercenary work is known as the Scar Market. It is an underground den of ramshackle dwellings for mercenaries and brokers who arrange work for these mercenaries. It is a tough and competitive place; there is only so much work to go around, and it goes first to those who make a name for themselves. 
}}

These "Booster" Mercenaries seek a name with their hand-crafted rocket skates, giving them high mobility at the cost of personal safety.

##### Rot Eater
These warriors might be the closest thing to a priesthood the Nachzehrer possess. All Nachzehrer have the ability to absorb the nutrients from dead Creatures, "consuming" them. For the Nachzehrer, this is a deeply spiritual and religious act; their death is not the end so long as they are consumed by another. Every Nachzehrer is nourished by they who came before; the Rot Eaters are those who have gorged themselves on Nachzehrer dead, becoming the embodiment of the cycle of decay and rebirth they subscribe to.

##### Wither Devourer
While all Nachzehrer can "consume" dead creatures to absorb their nutrients, these grotesque warriors take this "consuming" to an extreme. Their bodies are swollen and distended, and the stench of decay permeates from under the veil with which they cover their face. Woe betide those who find themselves grasped by those deceptively powerful arms, for they will glimpse the face underneath the veil, and the ravenous, gaping maw that dominates it.

:
The Devourer can only have one Creature swallowed at a time. While it has a Creature swallowed, this Creature is Resistant to Physical Damage dealt from outside of it, but its Speed is halved.
:
If the Devourer takes 30 or more Damage on a single Turn from a Creature inside of it, it must succeed on a DC20 Constitution Saving Throw or spit out the swallowed Creature at the end of the Turn, which falls Prone in a space within 10 feet of the Devourer. If the Devourer dies, it immediately spits out any swallowed Creature.

##### Deathveil Assassin
The Deathveil Assassins are shrouded in mystery. Matchless killers, they played an instrumental role in the regicide committed against King Theresa, the late King of Kazdel, on behalf of her brother, Theresis. These Assassins have abandoned their names and cut off their horns; they are absolutely fanatical believers in a destined fate, and see themselves as powerless to fight it. They do not consider themselves to be Sarkaz; simply agents of fate. 
:
The last Deathveil Assassins were dispatched to combat Victoria's Steam Knights during the occupation of Victoria; a mission from which they never returned. Or so the stories say.

{{wide

##### Sudaram
The "Sudaram", translated from old Sarkazian as "The Shroud of the Dead", are an elite but enigmatic group of the Nachzehrer King's most devoted followers. There is no organisation amongst them besides dedication to the King, Nezzalem; none sees themselves as greater than another. This attitude is representative of what it means to *be* a Sudaram, appearing so gaunt and withered where many Nachzehrer appear mighty or even bloated. The Sudaram are those that have truly taken on board what it means to be a Nachzehrer; those that understand that consumption and nourishment are two sides of the same coin, and who understand that simply consuming for its own sake is worthless. 
:
Once shining examples of Nachzehrer gluttony, the Sudaram now burn the nutrients they gathered to support their kin, in the hope that they might live long enough to achieve their own enlightenment. For the Sarkaz to survive, the means cannot be an end unto themselves.
}}

{{note
##### Encountering the Sudaram
As the Sudaram fills a role as a "Medic", they may underperform if encountered without Nachzehrer companions with which to heal.

#### Leader
:
##### Mercenary "Centurion"
Sarkaz mercenaries do not readily accept leadership unless you are paying them. For a member of that famously untrustful group to be considered worthy enough by their peers to lead them, you know their bond must be unbreakable, their strength absolute, and their mercy non-existent.

##### Goliath Worldcurser
As befitting their name, these so-called "Worldcursers" are a branch family of the more ubiquitous, but still rare Goliath Casters. The body of a Worldcurser is wracked with Oripathy, and thus all have a limited lease on life remaining; the Worldcursers choose to use that lease to cause as much suffering as possible for the highest pay, channelling their Arts through the Originium in their body, and spending the money they earn on fleeting pursuits for their fleeting life.

The number rolled on the d4 is the distance that bomb scatters; multiply the number rolled by ten. 
:

At the beginning of this Creature's next Turn, all of their fired Stickybombs explode; all Creatures within a 10-foot-radius sphere centred on each Stickybomb must make a DC 14 Dexterity Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 18 (4d8) Thunder Damage, or half as much (9) on a successful Save. If the Stickybomb is stuck to a Creature, that Creature automatically fails this Saving Throw.
:
This Attack does not require line of sight, but there can be no roof obstructing the arc of the stickybomb.

{{wide

##### Manfred, Supreme Commander
General Manfred is a famed commander serving the Sarkaz Royal Court, and was instrumental in the Kazdellian occupation of Londinium. He is young as Sarkaz of his rank go, approximately forty years old; for some, this is reason enough to look down upon him. However, his tactical acumen and personal strength have more than earned him his rank; many of his own men are fanatically loyal to him. In turn, he is fanatically loyal to Regent Theresis, who he has personally trained under since he was a young boy. While he has taken on board many lessons and gifts from the Regent, two are the most noteworthy; his incredible swordsmanship, and his unique Arts Unit, the *Teekazwurtzen*, which allows him to project bolts of energy at incredibly unpredictable angles by "bouncing" them off invisible beacons, as well as creating explosive detonations of air pockets.

{{monster,frame,wide

{{wide

##### Theresis, Regent of Kazdel
Theresis, the incumbent Regent of Kazdel after its previous king, his sister Theresa, was murdered by assassins on his orders. This is not a secret, nor is it considered a stain by many; Kazdel was in the midst of a bloody civil war between the two siblings, and the act restored the nation to peace. For all but the staunchest of Theresa loyalists, that was enough. He has not claimed the position of king, for he cannot; the Black Crown of Kazdel chooses its wearer, and it has not chosen him. Theresis is still the *de facto* leader of Kazdel, being the head of the Kazdel Military Commission, its ruling body, and has led Kazdel in its rebuilding and rearmament since the civil war.
:
He is a deeply cunning and level-headed leader, directly responsible for the machination of events that led to the Kazdellian occupation of Londinium, and the subsequent Londinium Crisis. If a manipulator was all he was, then a thousand Theresis could be found across Terra. Yet he is also the prize pupil of the Nachzehrer King, the "God of War", Nezzalem, an accolade which he holds for good reason. His swordsmanship is unmatched by all except the God of War himself, cutting great swathes even through the elite his enemies have to offer.

}}

### Reactions
***Shadow-walker.*** *Trigger:* Theresis is hit by an Attack, or is forced to make a Saving Throw against an effect he can see while there is a "Shadow of the Lord" within 60 feet of him. *Response:* Theresis swaps places with a "Shadow of the Lord" of his choice within 60 feet. If the triggering instance was an Attack, that Attack instead hits that "Shadow of the Lord". If the triggering instance was a Saving Throw, Theresis automatically succeeds on the Saving Throw if he has left the affected area; he takes no Damage and suffers no effects, but that "Shadow of the Lord" is treated as a new affected Creature.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}} 

{{wide

##### Duq'arael, The Sanguinarch
One of the oldest living beings on Terra, Duq'arael has a gore-soaked history longer than almost every nation on Terra, clocking in at nearly seven millennia. The blood of untold thousands stains his uncaring hands, including that of his own brother- once a previous King of Kazdel, slain by Duq'arael in a civil war over the King's pacifistic stance. The Sanguinarch's age and overwhelming strength have given rise to his overwhelming disdain towards almost everyone, especially those he sees as lesser. He is a man for whom "sanctity of life" has no meaning; abject terror follows him around like a pall, striking fear even into the hearts of the men and women who serve him. His legends are so cruel, and his attitude so dismissive, that he has even earned the ire of many of his fellow Vampires, and contemporaries among the royal court. Yet, precious few may gainsay the Crimson King and expect to live to see another dawn. Despite everything, he believes in the Regent's dream for the future of the Sarkaz, and is not above overwhelming atrocity to see it done.

{{monster,frame,wide

##### Nezzalem, Sarkaz God of War
Perhaps the oldest living Sarkaz, the name of Nezzalem is known across Terra. A legendary, revered figure to some, and a reviled, hated symbol of Sarkazian might to others, he predates the modern idea of warfare. Over millennia he has fought enemies uncounted, and has tasted his fair share of defeats and victories, yet Nezzalem remains. Even in the times where Kazdel has fallen again and again, the Nachzehrer King remains, consuming and growing in might, sustaining the regrowth of the Nachzehrer and by extension, Kazdel. What is frightening about Nezzalem, what has earned him his title as the God of War, is not his inherent might; it is his fortitude. War leaves nothing but ruin in its wake, but it does leave Nezzalem. 
:
His titles lead many to expect him to be a hot-blooded war hawk, which upon meeting him, is swiftly proven untrue. He certainly lusts for conflict and battle, but it is not a mindless desire, for he knows such things will come eventually, and he can patiently await the next great conflict. He is wise and stern, almost grandfatherly in some respects, and has trained and educated many generations of Kazdellian heroes; the previous King of Kazdel, her brother, and Patriot amongst them. He is also known to be passionate about agriculture, whiling away the time between conflicts with the growing of crops; perhaps another representation of the Nachzehrer view on the cycle of consumption and nourishment.

### Seaborn

The Seaborn are a non-human hive mind who dwell in the oceans of Terra; a terraforming project abandoned by The Predecessors during their fall. Their original purpose was to convert this barren world into a fertile one by removing Originium, and repurposing the nutrients found on the planet. To this end, they are hyper-adaptive, able to rapidly undergo evolution and speciation. 
:
All Seaborn belong to the hive mind, which refers to itself as "We Many", and work constantly to further its goals. All in We Many are equal. If there is not enough food, Seaborn will accept willingly being consumed by their comrades to cut the colony down to a manageable size, and feed them in the process. 
:
Despite their equality, Seaborn are not made equally. Seaborn come in all shapes and sizes, from the microscopic organisms that comprise the so-called "Nethersea Brand", to hulking sea monsters capable of smashing apart ships and soldiers. 

Some Seaborn possess advanced intelligence, able to learn and converse with humans and other species. Despite this, there are some concepts We Many cannot understand; even these advanced Seaborn are incapable of understanding emotional concepts such as art or morality; the concept of dishonesty is utterly foreign to them. 
:
The only Seaborn who are capable of these things are those humans who have become Seaborn, through assimilation; perhaps the most insidious facet of the Seaborn. Those who consume Seaborn, from the tiniest microbes to the flesh of the larger creatures, slowly transform into a member of We Many, evolving aquatic traits. The rate at which assimilation occurs correlates to the rate at which the Seaborn are consumed. When undergoing the transformation, only those of the strongest will maintain any of their individuality; all others become just another part of We Many. 
:
Some on Terra worship the Seaborn; a cult known as The Church of the Deep. Almost exclusively active within Iberia and Aegir, they seek for humankind to completely assimilate with We Many, seeing them as the next step of evolution. They believe that this is the only way Terran life can avoid the extraterrestrial Catastrophe foreseen by Martus the Sage, their founder; the very same Catastrophe that exterminated The Predecessors.

##### Bone Sea Drifter
A fish-like Seaborn created from the remains of dead Seaborn, they form an integral part of the Seaborn's wondrous ability to recycle. These Sea Drifters have very low metabolic costs, and travel in large packs to scavenge resources, both biological and mineral. They are willingly consumed by more advanced Seaborn, transferring their absorbed nutrients up the food chain.

{{note
##### The Nethersea Brand
While it appears to be a carpet of moss or algae, the "Nethersea Brand" is in actuality a rapidly growing colony of Seaborn micro-organisms that breaks down the surfaces onto which it spreads, converting them into nutrients for We Many. All Seaborn have a kind of symbiosis with the Brand, but some have a special relationship with it.
}}

##### Ridge Sea Spitter
Ridge Sea Spitters appear very similar to Deep Sea Sliders, perhaps sharing evolutionary ancestry; the main difference is in the arrangement of the bone structures around their head. While that of a slider forms a kind of protective cage for its head, that of the Spitter completely encloses it, like a closed flower-bud. This is not for protection; these overlapping plates form a pressure chamber that the spitter uses to project water with enough force to erode rock.

##### Basin Sea Reaper
Sea Reapers appear very similar to Sea Sliders; perhaps a more advanced form of that Seaborn species. They were evolved both to support Seaborn assaults on those they perceive as a threat, and to defend existing colonies.

##### Nethersea Founder
These strangely bipedal, exoskeletal Seaborn are responsible for founding new patches of Nethersea Brand, releasing the microbes inside them when their outer shell breaks down. Additionally, they have recently developed a second duty in response to efforts to contain the brand; protecting the foundling convoy from attack by dampening Originium Arts.

{{monster,frame

*Medium Aberration, Unaligned*

**Speed**       :: 30 ft, swim 30ft.

**Challenge**            :: 3 (700 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** +2}}
---
***Moving Colony.*** When this Creature dies, it spreads Nethersea Brand onto all non-living surfaces within 20 feet of it. Additionally, the area within 10 feet of this Creature is considered to be Nethersea Brand for anything that requires it.
:
***Warding Aura.*** All allied Creatures within 30 feet of this Creature are Resistant to Arts Damage. This effect is disabled if this Creature is Silenced.
:
***We Many.*** This Creature does not need to breathe, is Immune to the Charmed Condition (included in their Condition Immunities), and has Advantage on Charisma Saving Throws. Additionally, they are aware of what other Seaborn within 1 mile can see and hear.

***Shelled Strike.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 9 (1d12+2) Bludgeoning Damage.
:
Creatures hit must succeed on a DC13 Constitution Saving Throw or be Dazed until the end of their next Turn.
:
***Self-Detonation.*** All Creatures within 15 feet of this Creature must make a DC12 Dexterity Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 21 (6d6) Poison Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much (10) Damage. After using this Action, this Creature dies.

}}

\column

##### Nethersea Predator
These Creatures are the "White Blood Cells" of the Nethersea Brand; if there is a threat to the Brand, they are dispatched to dispose of it. They rely on their unnatural evasion and invisibility to dispatch their "prey".

##### Nethersea Swarmcaller
These Jellyfish-like Creatures are responsible for the evolution of the Nethersea Brand. They are essentially a living germination pool, a biological evolution lab; thus, even the very space around them is harmful, choked with the Brand. Even above water, the microscopic nature of the Brand means it can very quickly evolve wings.

{{monster,frame

*Large Aberration, Unaligned*

On a failed Save, they take 14 (4d6) Necrotic Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much (7) Damage. This Feature does not start active; this Creature must expend an Action to activate it. ***Activate Swarm Aura.*** This Creature disables or enables its Host of the Swarm Trait.

\column

##### Sea Crawler
The Seaborn do not produce perfectly clean work; many impurities are created and excavated during their terraforming process. The role of these Creatures is to absorb these impurities and slowly purify them through metabolic process. If they are damaged or punctured, however, these often-toxic compounds escape in a rush.

##### Primal Sea Piercer
A sister species to the Sea Reapers, Sea Piercers are the hunters of We Many. Biological life not necessary for assimilation is nutrients that can be used by We Many, and these Creatures are involved to bring those Creatures to an end; they have evolved a series of incredible sharp chitin "harpoons" within their head, and through an unusual muscle can launch these with fatal effect.

##### Nethersea Brandguider
These bipeds are formed whenever a colony of Nethersea Brand is in an environment it cannot survive; these Creatures gather the Brand into themselves and transport it to a new location. Even should they be hurt, they will drag themselves to the location, smearing the Brand as they go.

##### Tidelinked Archon
A large sea terror resembling a mantis shrimp who bears a split consciousness. While individually weak, unless both are slain simultaneously, they will be reborn from the other.

---
***Tidelink.*** When this Creature dies but is not destroyed while another Creature with this Ability is present within 300 feet, keep tracking this Creature's Turns. At the end of the third of its Turns after dying, if the other Creatures with this Ability are not also dead, this Creature is restored to life and its maximum Hit Points.
:

### Bonus Actions
***Aggressive.*** The Creature moves up to its Speed towards a hostile Creature it can see.

##### Nethersea Reefbreaker
A tremendously aggressive, vaguely humanoid coral monster born from the Nethersea Brand. Their role is to use their powerful frames and unusually hard bodies to smash surroundings that are unsuitable for sustaining We Many. Unfortunately, anything that doesn't belong to We Many falls under this heading.

A huge creature resembling a manta ray, it could be accurate to describe this creature as a "gunship". Evolved in response to increased human aggression against We Many, it is capable of hovering flight, is durable, and can rain down punishment on invaders; it is a force to be reckoned with.

##### Pathshaper
A humanoid, bipedal monstrosity that bears the characteristics of many water dwelling Creatures. It has been tasked by We Many to explore evolutionary pathways. When facing a problem, or a threat, it can rapidly divide fractally into a great many reflections of itself that have undergone a slightly different evolutionary route until it reaches a solution for the problem at hand. These shades appear to be mere shadows, but as the Iberian Inquisitions log of the fallen can attest to, they are very real.

{{wide

##### Nethersea Bishop
A true "Sea Monster", the Nethersea Bishop is a colossal cephalopodic Seaborn, with massive, sweeping tentacles and a head comprised of several massive eyestalks, such as those possessed by Deep Sea Sliders. No-one truly knows how or why these giants came to be, and for a blessing they remain extremely rare. It is suspected, however, that it arose from the perception and imagination regarding Seaborn from people assimilated into We Many; We Many does not imagine, and so perhaps could not tell the difference between what is and what could be. Regardless, when this beast climbs on shore alongside waves tainted with Nethersea Brand, it spells doom for all present.

{{monster,frame,wide
## Nethersea Bishop
*Gargantuan Aberration, Unaligned*
___
**Armor Class** :: 9 (Natural Armour)
**Hit Points**  :: 259 (14d20+112) 
**Speed**       :: 10 ft, swim 10ft.
___
|  STR  |  DEX  |  CON  |  INT  |  WIS  |  CHA  |
|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|
|18 (+4)|6 (-3) |26 (+8)|16 (+3)|18 (+4)|18 (+4)|
___
**Saving Throws**        :: Strength +8, Constitution +12, Wisdom +8
**Skills**               :: Athletics +8, Perception +8
**Damage Resistances**   :: Physical Damage
**Condition Immunities** :: Charmed, Frightened, Grappled, Prone, Restrained
**Senses**               :: passive Perception 18, Blindsight 300 ft
**Languages**            :: Understands spoken language but cannot speak
**Challenge**            :: 11 (7200 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** +4}}
---
***Evolution.*** While this Creature has less than half of its maximum Hit Points remaining, when it rolls to determine if its Great Tide Action recharges, it may add 1 to the result of that roll.
:
***Species Outbreak (1/day).*** If the Nethersea Bishop's Hit Points are reduced to 0, its Hit Point total becomes 130, and for the next hour, when it rolls to determine if its Great Tide Action recharges, it may add 1 to the result of that roll.
:
***We Many.*** This Creature does not need to breathe, is Immune to the Charmed Condition (included in their Condition Immunities), and has Advantage on Charisma Saving Throws. Additionally, they are aware of what other Seaborn within 1 mile can see and hear.

\column

### Actions
***Multiattack.*** This Creature makes one Attack and uses Great Tide or Spread Brand.
:
***Tentacle.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +8 to hit, reach 15ft., one target. *Hit:* 17 (2d12+4) Bludgeoning Damage.
:
A Creature hit by this Attack is knocked back 10 feet.
:
***Great Tide (Recharge 4+).*** All non-Seaborn Creatures within a 100-foot-radius of the Nethersea Bishop must make a DC 16 Strength Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 7 (2d6) Bludgeoning Damage; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage (3). Damage dealt by this Action is considered to be Arts Damage.
:
***Spread Brand.*** The Nethersea Bishop spreads the Nethersea Brand onto all surfaces within a 20-foot-radius sphere centred on a point it can see within 100 feet.
:
***Tectonic Shake (Recharge 5+).*** The Nethersea Bishop chooses a location it can see within 100 feet; all non-Seaborn Creatures within a 20-foot-radius of that point must make a DC 16 Strength Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 14 (4d6) Bludgeoning Damage and are knocked Prone. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage (7), and are not knocked Prone. Damage dealt by this Action is considered to be Arts Damage.

### Legendary Actions
Legendary Action Uses: 1. Immediately after another Creature’s turn, This Creature can expend a use to take one of the following actions. This Creature regains all expended uses at the beginning of its Turn. 
:
***Scions of the Nethersea (only while below half of maximum Hit Points).*** :: Roll 1d4; the Nethersea Bishop spawns a Creature in an unoccupied space it can see that is either covered in Nethersea Brand or submerged in water based on the result. **1**- Floating Sea Drifter, **2**- Nethersea Founder, **3**- Nethersea Swarmcaller, **4**- Nethersea Predator.
***We Many.*** :: The Nethersea Bishop spawns 1d6 Nethersea Spewers in unoccupied spaces it can see that are either covered in Nethersea Brand or submerged in water.
}}

{{wide

##### The Endspeaker, Will of We Many

Despite its unassuming appearance, the Endspeaker is responsible for the sinking of the pride of Iberia's navy, the Stultifera Navis. All Seaborn adapt rapidly, as is their trait as a terraforming device, but the Endspeaker adapts at a truly horrifying speed, learning not just from its own experiences, but those of its kin, and even other organisms it consumes. Thus, the Endspeaker can undergo years of Seaborn evolution in moments; when combined with its surprising fortitude and resilience, it makes for a fearsome foe. The Endspeaker is the archetypal Seaborn; everything a Seaborn was designed to be, distilled into a single Creature, created by happenstance. 

}}

## Endspeaker, Will of We Many
*Large Aberration, Unaligned*
___

___
|  STR  |  DEX  |  CON  |  INT  |  WIS  |  CHA  |
|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|
|18 (+4)|18 (+4)|14 (+2)|16 (+3)|18 (+4)|16 (+4)|
___
**Saving Throws**        :: Dexterity +8, Wisdom +8, Charisma +8
**Skills**               :: Athletics +8, Perception +8
**Condition Immunities** :: Charmed, Frightened, Stunned
**Senses**               :: passive Perception 18, Blindsight 300 ft
**Languages**            :: Telepathy 100 ft.
**Challenge**            :: 14 (11500 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** +4}}
---
***Consume and Adapt (2/day).*** When the Endspeaker's Hit Points are reduced to 0, its Hit Point total becomes 130. In addition, it absorbs the nearest Directed Evolution Marker, gaining access to special Traits and Actions based on what Creature died to create that marker. It loses access to these Traits at the next dawn.
:
***Directed Evolution.*** When a "Nethersea" Seaborn dies within 150 feet of the Endspeaker, a Directed Evolution Marker corresponding to that Creature at the location where it died. Directed Evolution Markers are completely intangible, invisible, and invulnerable, and only function for this Creature's other Traits.
:
***Founder's Blessing (Founder Marker absorbed).*** This Creature is Resistant to Arts Damage.
:
***Hardened Carapace (Brandguider Marker absorbed).*** This Creature is Resistant to Physical Damage.
:
***Host of the Swarm (Swarmcaller Marker absorbed).*** The Endspeaker continually projects dangerous micro-organisms in a 10-foot radius around it. When this area enters a non-Seaborn Creature’s space, a non-Seaborn Creature enters the area during its Turn, or a non-Seaborn Creature ends its Turn in the area, that Creature must make a DC 15 Constitution Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 14 (4d6) Necrotic Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much (7) Damage.
:
***In Lantern's Light (Brandguider Marker absorbed).*** This Creature has 100 ft. of True Sight.
:
***Protective Cloud (Swarmcaller Marker absorbed).*** The Nethersea Brand in the area around this Creatures fouls vital strikes. Attacks against this Creature cannot benefit from Advantage.

\column

***Toxin Secretion (Spewer Marker absorbed).*** When the Endspeaker hits a Creature with an Attack, that Creature must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution Saving Throw or have Nervous Impairment until the end of their next Turn. A Creature that succeeds on this Saving Throw is unaffected by this Trait until the beginning of the Endspeaker's next Turn.
:
***We Many.*** This Creature does not need to breathe, is Immune to the Charmed Condition (included in their Condition Immunities), and has Advantage on Charisma Saving Throws. Additionally, they are aware of what other Seaborn within 1 mile can see and hear.

### Actions
***Multiattack.*** The Endspeaker makes three Melee Attacks.
:
***Predator Blades (Predator Marker absorbed).*** +8 to hit, reach 5ft., one target. *Hit:* 22 (4d8+4) Slashing Damage.
:
***Slam.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +8 to hit, reach 5ft., one target. *Hit:* 13 (2d8+4) Bludgeoning Damage.
:
***Spine Shot.*** *Ranged Weapon Attack:* +8 to hit, range 60/100 ft., one target. *Hit:* 18 (4d6+4) Piercing Damage.
:
***Spine Spew (Spewer Marker absorbed).*** +8 to hit, range 60/100 ft., up to three targets. *Hit:* 18 (4d6+4) Poison Damage.
:
This Attack ignores the benefits of Half and Three-quarters Cover.

### Bonus Actions
***Spread Brand (Founder Marker absorbed) (Recharge 4+).*** Nethersea Brand covers all surfaces in a 20-foot-radius of the Endspeaker.

### Reactions
***Hunting Stealth (Predator Marker absorbed) (Recharge 4+).*** This Creature becomes Invisible until the end of its next Turn. It loses this Invisibility after it deals Damage to a Creature.

### Lair Actions
At the beginning of every Round, this Creature can take one of the following Lair Actions if encountered in its Lair. Once it has used a Lair Action, it cannot use that Lair Action again until it has used every other Lair Action.
:
***Call Lesser Nethersea Scions.*** :: The Endspeaker summons 1d6 Nethersea Spewers in unoccupied spaces it can see that are either covered in Nethersea Brand or submerged in water.

***Call Nethersea Scion.*** :: Roll 1d6: the Endspeaker summons a Creature in an unoccupied space it can see that is either covered in Nethersea Brand or submerged in water. **1-3**- Nethersea Founder, **4-6**- Nethersea Swarmcaller.

***Call Greater Nethersea Scion.*** :: Roll 1d6: the Endspeaker summons a Creature in an unoccupied space it can see that is either covered in Nethersea Brand or submerged in water. **1-3**- No Creature summoned, **4-5**- Nethersea Predator, **6**- Nethersea Brandguider.

{{note
##### Authorised Caster Subtypes
Authorised Casters, much like Characters of the Caster Class, have elemental specialisations. For each Authorised Caster, choose one of the following Arts Pools; this determines what Arts they can cast using their Spellcasting Feature.
:
**Corrosion**

*At Will:* Produce Acid, Corrode, Acid Douse

*1/day each:* Acid Arrow (3rd Level), Melting Mist
:
**Crude**

*At Will:* Prestidigitation, Shield, Magic Missile

*1/day each:* Magic Missile (3rd Level), Missile Barrier
:
**Earth**

*At Will:* Mending, Mold Earth, Earth Tremor

*1/day each:* Maximillian's Earthen Grasp, Erupting Earth
:
**Electric**

*At Will:* Shocking Grasp, Lightning Spear, Witchbolt

*1/day each:* Galvanic Gauntlet, Lightning Bolt
:
**Flame**

*At Will:* Produce Flame, Protect from Cold, Burning Hands

*1/day each:* Scorching Ray (3rd Level), Fireball
:
**Fluid**

*At Will:* Aqua Blade, Shape Water, Create/Destroy Water

*1/day each:* Misty Step, Water Pulse
:
**Frost**

*At Will:* Protect from Heat, Armour of Agathys, Ice Knife

*1/day each:* Rime's Binding Ice (3rd Level), Icicle Crash
:
**Light**

*At Will:* Light, Guiding Bolt, Silent Image

### Ursus

The Empire of Ursus was, or perhaps still is based on your perspective, a military juggernaut. Since the days of the Hippogryph Empire, the metal-clad boots of Ursus have trampled rough-shod over Terra's northern regions. 

##### Infected Patrol Officer
Ursus' treatment of the Infected is famously awful. While many nations are content to simply leave the Infected to die in their own little holes, Ursus hunts these unfortunates and forces them to work in mines, under brutal conditions. When they die, they are thrown into an unmarked grave, and that's that. These soldiers are the strong-arm of the organisation responsible for uncovering and arresting Infected; half detective, half executioner. While they do not roam the country in huge armies, there is always just enough to ensure that no-one dares raise a hand to them.

Wielding massive custom made spearguns, they fire them into their foe as they charge forward, disrupting enemy ranks before laying about with the massive axe-like blades on those same spearguns. The Imperial Strikers are Ursus; pure, utilitarian brutality personified.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}  

##### Emperor's Blade
As the name suggests, the Emperor's Blades are a unit within the Ursus Royal Guard who answer only to the Emperor. Across the world, the title of "Emperor's Blade" is spoken of with fear and awe; stories told in hushed tones of the horrendous deeds for which they have been responsible. Entire settlements wiped clean, a city sunk into a lake, and massacres uncounted. They do not tire, and their martial skill is second to almost none, but it is not these things that make them truly frightening. They are fearsome for their seemingly supernatural abilities; cutting with shadows, and secreting a strange black mist that shatters the mind of those exposed to it. 
:
Unknown to all except a select few, the Emperor's Blades are created through a ritual in which a member of the Royal Guard are fused with fragments of Collapsals; this fusion is the source of their supernatural power. The "black mist" they secrete is the very same as the "Contamination" of the Collapsals, though while the Blade remains, they can exert control over it, referring to it as their "Dominion". Amongst those that even know of the existence of the Blades, there is a saying; "Every Royal Guard is as a dominion; the land beneath their feet is all the territory of Ursus."
:
This demonic pact comes with a cost; while they need no sustenance, each Blade is sealed forever in their armour, as it helps them contain the influence of the Collapsals. When their armour is breached, this Contamination escapes explosively, permanently damaging the world around them. During the disastrous Ursus-Higashi War, twenty Emperor's Blades lost their lives in a place known then as the Valley of the Setting Sun; a location now so tainted by Collapsal Corruption as to have vanished from consensus reality.
:
An Emperor's Blade is a very rare sight, and portends nothing good. No-one outside of the Royal Guard knows how many there truly are, as most operate independently or in small cells. They are the ultimate agents of Ursus.

{{note
##### Running an Emperor's Blade
Each Emperor's Blade is a small army unto themselves, and utterly inhuman in nature, but they should not be considered as mindless and violent. An Emperor's Blade is an agent of Ursus as much as a warrior; in essence, they are super powered spies. They could be found anywhere on Terra, carrying out missions for their own ends or for Ursus. They will kill if needed, but it is typically not their first recourse.
:
As such, an Emperor's Blade can work as a great recurring villain; what begins as a chance encounter could build to a climactic confrontation.

}}

<img
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/a/a8/Emperor%27s_Blade.png?7f0306&20230927001947'
  style='position:absolute;top:50px;right:-275px;width:1000px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

**Armor Class** :: 14 (Emperor's Blade Suit)
**Hit Points**  :: 153 (18d8+72)
**Speed**       :: 30ft.
___
|  STR  |  DEX  |  CON  |  INT  |  WIS  |  CHA  |
|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|
|18 (+4)|16 (+3)|18 (+4)|12 (+1)|20 (+5)|20 (+5)|
___
**Saving Throws**        :: Strength +8, Wisdom +9, Charisma +9
**Damage Resistances**   :: Physical Damage
**Skills**               :: Athletics +7, Lore +9, Intimidation +9
**Senses**               :: passive Perception 15
**Languages**            :: Speaks Ursine + three other languages
**Challenge**            :: 11 (7200 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** +4}}
---
***Damage Reduction.*** This Creature has 5 DR against all Damage.
:
***Dominion of Ursus.*** At the end of the Blade's Turn, Dominion spreads within a 10-foot-radius of the Blade. The Blade can remove any Dominion in a 10-foot-radius around themselves as an Action. Other Creatures who begin their Turn within Dominion take 5 (1d8) Psychic Damage.
:
***Legendary Resistance (3/Day)***. If this Creature fails a Saving Throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
:
***Setting Sun.*** When the Blade is reduced to 0 Hit Points, its inner Dominion explodes outwards in a 30-foot-radius. All Creatures in the area must make a DC 17 Dexterity Saving Throw, taking 29 (6d8) Psychic Damage on a failed Save, or half as much (14) on a successful Save. The affected area is the considered to be Dominion. 
:
***Shield of Shadows.*** When the Blade would take Damage while within Dominion, it can halve that Damage; once it has used this Trait, it cannot do so again until the beginning of its next Turn.

\column

### Actions
***Multiattack.*** This Creature makes two Melee Attacks. It can replace one of them with a use of Collapsing Fear or Shadow Slash.
:
***Collapsing Fear (Art) (Recharge 5+).*** All other Creatures within a 100-foot-radius of the Blade who are within Dominion must make a DC 17 Charisma Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 23 (5d8) Psychic Damage and are Incapacitated until the end of their next Turn as psychic terror overwhelms them. On a successful Save, they take half as much (11) Damage and are not Incapacitated.
:
***Emperor's Blade.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +8 to hit, reach 5ft., one target, *Hit:* 13 (2d8+4) Slashing Damage plus 9 (2d8) Psychic Damage if the Blade is within Dominion. 
:
***Shadow Slash (Art).*** *Ranged Spell Attack:* +8 to hit, range 60 ft., one target, *Hit:* 21 (3d10+4) Psychic Damage.
:
Reactions cannot be taken in response to the Attack Roll or Damage Roll for this Attack.

### Bonus Actions
***Conqueror (Art) (Recharge 4+).*** The Blade causes an explosive spread of Dominion in a 20-foot-radius sphere centred on a point they can see within 100 feet. All Creatures within the area must make a DC 17 Dexterity Saving Throw; on a successful Save, they take 9 (2d8) Psychic Damage, or half as much (4) on a successful Save. The affected area is then considered to be Dominion.

### Reactions
***Parry.*** *Trigger:* The Blade is hit by an Attack while holding a weapon. *Response:* The Blade adds 4 to its AC against that attack, possibly causing it to miss. 
}}

{{wide

### Uncategorized

The following Creatures are those that do not fit neatly into an established category, but were still important to include for one reason or another.

##### Automaton
Terra's grasp of autonomous robotics far excceds many of their other fields; these machines are the epitome of that concept. Far more than simple drones to be remote controlled, these are semi-autonomous robots, sometimes equipped with advanced Artificial Intelligence.

}}

{{monster,frame
## Automaton (Platform)
*Normal Small Construct (Drone), Neutral Alignment*
___
**Armor Class** :: 12
**Hit Points**  :: 25
**Speed**       :: 30 ft.
___
|  STR  |  DEX  |  CON  |  INT  |  WIS  |  CHA  |
|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|
|12 (+1)|10 (+0)|14 (+2)|4 (-4) |4 (-4) |1 (-5) |
___
**Damage Immunities**    :: Poison, Necrotic
**Condition Immunities** :: Charmed, Frightened, Poisoned, Necrotised, Nervous Impairment
**Senses**               :: Darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 9
**Languages**            :: Understands the languages of its creator.
**Challenge**            :: 2 (450 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** +2}}
___

***Durable Construction*.** This Creature has a DR of 3 against Physical Damage.
:
***High Horsepower.*** If this Creature moves at least 15 feet in a straight line towards a Creature immediately before hitting it with a Ram Attack, it can push that Creature 10 feet directly away if it is no more than one size larger than the Platform.
:
***Can't Climb.*** This Creature has no arms, and moves with wheels, tracks, or small legs; it can traverse most ground terrain, but cannot climb anything larger than a step.

### Actions

***Ram.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, Reach 5ft, one target. *Hit* 7 (1d8 + 2) Bludgeoning Damage.

### Reactions
***Coming Through.*** *Trigger:* This Creature has moved at least 15 feet in a straight line immediately before this. *Response:* This Creature makes a Ram Attack.
:
***Protector Protocol.*** *Trigger:* A Creature within 5 feet of the Platform (other than the Platform) would make an Attack Roll. *Response:* The Platform imposes Disadvantage on that Attack.

}}

***Portable.*** As an Action, an allied Creature within 5 feet of the Turret can fold it into a portable form; while it is in this Portable form, it is considered to be Unconscious. While in portable form, the Turret weighs 10 lbs, and is small enough to be carried under one arm. A Creature carrying the Turret can unfold it as a Bonus Action, deploying it to an unoccupied space within 5 feet of them.

##### Lateran Executor
It's rarely good news to see a Lateran Executor. At best, they have come to inform you of some terrible news, or to serve you some papers. At worst, you have wronged Laterano, and they have come to right that wrong.

## Patch Notes

{{descriptive
##### Version 1.3.1: Should Probably have been v1.4
- The additional Damage granted to all Cantrips now only applies to the first Damage Roll of a Cantrip per Turn.
- Caprinae Species Feature Arts Resistance is now a passive effect instead of being 1/Short Rest.
- Caprinae Species Feature In Their Blood minor rework; now scales more aggressively with level, and provides you the Arts immediately.
- Djall Sarkaz Subspecies added.
- Ku'kul'kan Rare Species added.
- Lung Rare Species added.
- Advanced Caster Skill "Precise Redirection" now specifies what Actions are required to interact with sections of that Skill.
- Core Caster Feature "Master of the Elements" has had the Damage Resistance granted by Spatial Arts from Force to Thunder.
- Fortress Defender Feature "Barrage" now allows the ignoring of the Brace Property.
- Fortress Defender Feature "Danger Close" no longer allows you to exclude yourself.
- Guard Class Feature "Unrelenting" now only allows for 1 Attack instead of 1 Action.
- Guard Class Feature "Unrelenting Upgraded" now allows for an Action or Bonus Action by expending both Unrelenting Charges.
- Artblade Guard Feature "Unleash" is now optional to activate.
- Centurion Guard has recieved a minor rework.
- - "Brutal Execution" now has a Saving Throw attached, and Frightens enemies who fail it.
  - "Mow Them Down" no longer allows you to make Opportunity Attacks at disengaging enemies, but instead allows you to throw defeated enemies at their allies.
  - "Cull The Meek" has been replaced with "Guillotine", a powerful single target attack to give Centurion *something* to do when there are no minions to fight.
  - "Shredder" base damage has been reduced from 1d6+Strength Modifier to Strength Modifier. However, it is doubled against Creatures Frightened of you.
  - "Storm of Blood" now provides the Reaction-refreshing that "Cull The Meek" used to.
- Crusher Guard Feature "Foecrusher" no longer provides 1d10 additional Damage. The additional Damage remains on "Overbear" but may be removed in future.
- Crusher Guard Feature "Heroic Blow" now allows the rerolling of any damage dice of your choice.

}}

\column

{{descriptive
- Duellist Guard Engagements have been adjusted for better flow.
  - Balestra no longer requires a Bonus Action, but now requires you to actually move the provided distance to gain the DR ignoring effect.
  - Fleche now requires a Bonus Action to prevent the Declare Opponent > Fleche easy kill for Normal Enemies.
  - Redoublement now halves the Damage of the affected Attack after use; Feint is now the better option for securing that finishing blow, where Redoublement is for when you really need a particular Attack to hit (such as a Displacement or Derobement)
- Liberator Guard Feature "Guide The Masses" now can only be used in combat.
- Lord Guard Feature "Force of Will" has had its Damage lowered across the board as it was overperforming.
- Weaponmaster Guard Feature "Martial Mastery" no longer provides additional dice of damage, but Xd6 Damage for different Mastery Properties, for parity across weapons.
- Weaponmaster Guard Feature "Steelwind" has been reworded for (hopefully) improved clarity. It still functions the same way.
- Anti-Air Marksman Feature "Shoot To Kill" Damage has been reduced to 1d4, or 1d6 beyond 15th Level.
- Deadeye Marksman has had a small rework.
  - "Deadshot" no longer provides additional Weapon Damage while in Deadshot state, instead providing a minimum roll of 3 on all Damage Dice for Attacks made during Deadshot state.
  - "Kingslayer" renamed to "Target Shooting", and now provides +1 Crit Range against your Target, or +2 against Targets who are an Elite/Leader. Additionally, it allows you to declare a target in advance as a Short Rest Action without expending your Target Lock Feature, if you know their name or enough distinguishing Features.
  - "Vital Strike" damage lowered to 2d6 from 3d6, but now scales to 3d6 at 7th Level and 4d6 at 14th Level.
  - "Anti-Sniper Training" now provides Stealth benefits if you are in Cover.
- Flinger Marksman Feature "Weapon Binding" now provides your second Bound Weapon at 5th Level instead of 7th. Your third weapon remains at 14th Level.
- Heavyshot Marksman Feature "Savage Shots" allows you to reroll any number of individual dice instead of the whole Damage Roll. Let's go gambling!
- Spreadshot Marksman has been completely reworked.
- Witchcraft Medic Feature "Efficient Life Support" now provides Life Support based on Arts Points expended, rather than level of the cast Art.
- Infuser Specialist Feature "New Concoctions" Bungarotoxin now deals 2d10 Poison Damage irrespective of the Creature passing or failing the Saving Throw.
- Haemo/Necro/Neurotoxin now inflict reduced Damage when the Saving Throw is passed.
- The Deadly Fighter Fighting Style has been changed to provide +1 Critical Range on your first Attack with a Deadly Weapon each Turn, rather than adding your Proficiency Bonus to the Damage.
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}} 

{{descriptive


##### Version 1.3: Rare Species
- Rare Species have been introduced; these will not be suitable for every table, but will be useful for some. The currently available rare species are:
  - Abyssal Hunter
  - Draco
  - Elf
  - Nightzmoran Kuranta
- Oni Species added.
- Nachzehrer Sarkaz Subspecies added. 
- Changes to Mettle generation to prevent abuse; Mettle not generated from a Death Saving Throw is now granted at the end of the Turn. 
- New Special Arts Pool: Necrotic Arts
  - 7 custom Arts added for this Pool
- New Special Arts Pool: Elven Magic
  - 6 custom Arts added for this Pool
- Minor error fixes.  
  

##### Version 1.2: The Big Rework Patch
This is a massive patch, probably the biggest there will be for a while. The notes are too lengthy to record here; if you're interested in reading the full notes, you can find them [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1aInpEA_2RSgjFuNKRf2VtbA0rMvSEcVskri13xRaMNs/edit?usp=sharing). As of writing this, they are messy; that will be fixed and this notice removed when that is done. Some highlights include: 
- Cantrip Damage now scales with Spellcasting Ability Modifier.
- Healing can now generate Skill Points.
- Many changes to Character Origins designed to cut down on tracked resources.
- Guard Class rework, including all of its Branches.
- More than 10 non-Guard Branches have been reworked.
- Arts Points have been increased by roughly 50% across the board.
- Many rules have been clarified, with precedents and examples provided where necessary.
- Acknowledgements Updated.


##### Version 1.1
- Spelling and Grammar fixes.
- Character Sheet added.
- Acknowledgements updated.

##### Version 1.0
- Released.
}}
